The six-hundred-and-eighteenth research cycle is complete. It is a **no-change cycle in outcome, and a record-repair cycle in substance**. The recorded external constraint was re-read first, and it is **superseded**: the carry-forward text in every handoff still asserts that the Binance archive serves "HTTP 200 static front page only; /data/ returns HTTP 404, no traversable usable datasets", but this cycle verified at object level that concrete archive objects return HTTP 200 with readable payloads. The stale carry-forward is therefore the defect this cycle repaired, and the access premise recorded in the ninth-cycle-through-919th-cycle lineage is withdrawn.

**What was verified, at object level, this cycle (2026-11-18T07:18–07:25Z, single bounded probe set, statuses recorded once):**
- `data/futures/um/daily/klines/BTCUSDT/1d/BTCUSDT-1d-2024-01-01.zip` → **200, 353 B**; `.../BTCUSDT-1d-2026-11-17.zip` → **200**; the kline tree is current to at least **2026-11-17**, not 2026-11-10.
- `data/futures/um/daily/premiumIndexKlines/BTCUSDT/1d/BTCUSDT-1d-2024-01-01.zip` → **200, 326 B**.
- `data/futures/um/daily/metrics/BTCUSDT/BTCUSDT-metrics-2026-11-10.zip` → **200, 11,720 B**, `LastModified 2026-11-11T06:34:33Z`, **289 rows** (5-minute cadence), header `create_time,symbol,sum_open_interest,sum_open_interest_value,count_toptrader_long_short_ratio,sum_toptrader_long_short_ratio,count_long_short_ratio,sum_taker_long_short_vol_ratio`. Metrics current to at least **2026-11-17**.
- `data/futures/um/monthly/fundingRate/BTCUSDT/BTCUSDT-fundingRate-2024-01.zip` → **200, 696 B, 94 rows**; 2026-09 and 2026-10 → **200**; 2026-11 → **404** (month not yet closed).
- Relocated USDⓈ-M liquidations `data3/liquidationSnapshot/BTCUSDT/` via S3 ListObjectsV2 → **562 objects = 281 zips + 281 sidecars, 6,427,695 B total**, all `LastModified` 2025-04-16T06:43:44–49Z, **exactly bounded 2023-06-25..2024-03-31**: 2023-06-25 and 2024-03-31 return 200, 2024-04-01 and later return **404**.
- COIN-M `data/futures/cm/daily/liquidationSnapshot/BTCUSD_PERP/` → 2024-10-13 and **2024-10-14 return 200**, 2024-10-15 returns **404**. Inverse arm ends 2024-10-14, confirmed.
- S3 ListObjectsV2 `?list-type=2&prefix=data/&delimiter=/` → **200, KeyCount 3, three prefixes** `data/futures/`, `data/spot/`, `data/option/`. This corrects the SOURCE_NOTES claim of "root listing 17 keys": the count of 17 appears nowhere in the listing, and 1,000 keys (truncated) are returned only when the delimiter is omitted.
- Delisted-symbol klines remain free and dated: `LUNAUSDT` 2022-01-01 → **200**, 2024-01-01 and 2026-11-10 → **404**, consistent with the recorded terminal-file rule.
- **New this cycle, answering the open question the 920th cycle left:** OKX `GET /api/v5/public/funding-rate-history?instId=BTC-USDT-SWAP` ignores `page`, `after`, `begin` and `end` — every page from 1 to 320 returns the **same 278–279 rows spanning 2026-08-17 to 2026-11-18 (~93 days)**. OKX `GET /api/v5/public/liquidation-orders?instType=SWAP&instFamily=BTC-USDT&state=filled` returns side-attributed events (`side`/`posSide`/`sz`/`bkPx`/`ts`, grouped by `instId` with `details[]`, `totalLoss`, `uly`) but retains only **~22.9 hours** (page 1: 25,600 events; page 2: 10,864; page 3: empty; oldest 2026-11-17T07:54:20Z). **OKX therefore does not create history and does not reach the post-2024 regime.** The 920th cycle's "next question" is now closed in the negative.
